Research program · Frozen complete grammar/receptor feasibility · Earlier accepted left-successor resource study

Eight exact local-interface workspace estimates

OutcomeSelection and exact requestsProposed geometryCosts and limitsEvidence

All eight separately selected exact local-interface requests completed capacity estimation and full independent fixed-program/heap/encoding replay.

This is preparation only. There have been zero new native queries or target searches. The 93 fresh native-interface admissions remain unresolved, and 86 of the 94 complete inventory requests have not had their capacity estimated. No commutativity proof has been selected or checked.

The next target remains \(\forall\,\mathit{x}\;\left(\forall\,\mathit{y}\;\left(\left(\mathit{x}+\mathit{y}\right)=\left(\mathit{y}+\mathit{x}\right)\right)\right)\). The complete five-node grammar and the two actually discovered lemma certificates remain unchanged. This supplement checks only resource feasibility for a bounded generic pilot; it supplies no target-proof path, selected induction variable, learned cluster policy or RL result.

Seven selected local requests fit within the original 73,178-cell heap band. The newly wrapped left-successor theorem certificate requires 146,733 cells. The common proposed physical heap capacity is 262,144 cells, covering the largest complete estimate plus 64 spare blanks. This has now been independently estimated for these actual requests; it was not inherited as an assumption from the earlier theorem study.

Generic size-stratified selection, frozen before outcomes

All 94 exact retained-union admission requests participate in selection. Partition by operation and equation origin, then select the largest canonical whole-request JSON byte count in each nonempty stratum; break size ties by the maximal exact definition name. The eight winners, their entire requests and hashes were frozen before any estimate. A representative induction interface is chosen solely by this declared size rule; the complete grammar still contains both induction alternatives.

The target theorem is not supplied as a proof sequence. Native checking must validate the whole local definition before its block interface can be trusted. The accepted primitive theorem certificate alone does not automatically admit the newly wrapped local-definition request.

Unchanged parser, checker and palette; proposed blank workspace

The fixed tree instruction program completed each exact selected request. A separately written stack interpreter independently reproduced every instruction/event/profile and every canonical heap node. Record widths are calculated exactly, including the sentinel and trailing blank. These complete deterministic heap estimates do not certify any unselected request or every other tape band, and do not replace native microcode execution.

Other bandsHeap band 26Call stack 28Fixed assertion 29Free certificate 30

Only the proposed external blank capacity of heap band 26 changes from 73,178 to 262,144. Every initial word and local head stays byte-identical; later physical bands move by the added 188,966 blanks. The independent auditor reconstructs every original and proposed encoded input byte and physical band displacement. No semantic checker instruction, symbol alphabet, parser, universal tile palette or mathematical premise changes.

Separate resource ledger

ComponentSecondsPeak RSS bytesNative queries
Eight selected fixed-program capacity estimates19.926487,948,2880
Independent complete selected-request replay/encoding19.841522,076,1600

Both processes used a 180-second / 512 MiB ceiling. Each selected estimate had a 20-million tree-instruction and 200,000-node ceiling; proposed physical heap capacity was capped at 1,048,576 cells. No ceiling was raised and no native admission batch started. These are shared-host resource-study measurements, not a cluster-search speed claim.

The first complete grammar attempt’s memory-budget unknown, fresh retry and independent audit costs remain in the immutable feasibility ledger. All prior zero-left and left-successor theorem discovery, admission, certification and unknown costs remain in their existing readers. The separate entire-catalog retry correspondence inspection adds 5.479 seconds; it confirms unchanged grammar payloads except measured build time and a descriptive compaction tag. Manual infrastructure work remains unmeasured.

Any small native pilot is a separately frozen next gate after this audit. It must use these exact reconstructed inputs, preserve unknown/rejected outcomes and account for complete independent native replay. The unselected 86 capacity estimates and all unresolved native interfaces remain explicit; no 93-query batch is authorized by this supplement.
